# SGW_Dakara_Multi-Visit_Dev_Master_v1

Markdown rendering of `SGW_Dakara_Multi-Visit_Dev_Master_v1.xlsx` from the SGW Claude Server Handoff Pack v1.2.
Generated by `xlsx_to_md.py`; cell values only (no formulas, formatting or comments).
Sheet order and headers are preserved verbatim. Evidence labels inside the
sheets control authority, per the pack's `docs/SOURCE_POLICY.md`.

## 00_README

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Workbook | SGW Dakara — Multi-Visit Dev Master v1 |
| Scope | Dakara across three distinct production phases: prologue/starter Dakara_E1, middle Dakara_E2, and late Dakara_E3 + Superweapon. Runtime content is separated by phase so missions/NPCs/state do not bleed between visits. |
| Six progression paths | Asgard, Free Jaffa, SGU Human, Goa'uld, Loyalist Jaffa, OP-Core Human. This workbook treats these as the six campaign/path columns used by the progression sheet; it does not claim a one-to-one mapping to internal ArchetypeId class definitions. |
| Phase 1 — Prologue / E1 | Free Jaffa has a source-backed Dakara starter-era chain (1645-1655). SGU Human visits Dakara in Mission 1570 SG-18. Dakara_E1 is also explicitly called the 'Starter instance for Jaffa' in Planets & Locations. |
| Phase 2 — Praxis / E2 | The middle Praxis-side chain centers on Mission 388 Dakara in Chaos and Rohurk/Praxis infiltration. The same raw E2 cache also contains a separate SGU-side chain beginning 400 Dakara Under Siege; it is retained in its own tab because the coarse progression aid does not show SGU at Dakara in this phase. |
| Phase 3 — all six / E3 | Praxis paths use 1379-1384 as the recovered main chain; SGU paths use 1385-1390. Side missions 1393-1406 sit around those two alliance branches. This matches the later progression point where all six columns visit Dakara under 'Straegis Unleashed'. |
| Worlds | Dakara base World24; MissionTestDakara30; Dakara_E1=61; E1 Story Room=62; Dakara_E2=63; Dakara_E3=64; Dakara_Superweapon=65. |
| Gate records | Separate cooked Stargates survive for E1, E2 and E3. All share the same XYZ/yaw but have distinct addresses/origin sequence values and separate prefab names. |
| Map reality | Full production UMAP/MapData + 400+ streaming cells survive for Dakara_E1 and E1 Story Room. In the audited handoff, E2/E3 survive as WorldInfo + Stargate + minimaps, but no named production E2/E3 UMAP/MapData files were found. |
| Evidence rule | Raw cooked missions/dialogs/interactions/worlds/gates outrank the progression aid and secondary spreadsheets. User-established route information is marked USER-CONFIRMED. Gaps remain visible. |
| Important branch rule | Do not merge the Praxis E3 and SGU E3 story chains. They converge on stopping the Superweapon but use different political leadership, handoffs and offworld command structures. |
| Legacy rule | Missions 394/395 are labeled DELETE. in the cooked cache; Mission 413 is an older Superweapon breach record. They are preserved only as legacy/conflict evidence. |
| Snapshot | 2026-09-18. |

_13 data rows, 2 columns._

| 1633 | 109393 | 941 | Colonel Marsh | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | Is a mission to Dakara even feasible given Ra's invasion?<br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109394 | 945 | Moh'katan | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | That is unknown. The Free Jaffa have many resources backing Dakara. My confederate Rohurk informs me they have prepared for siege. Much depends on the size of Ra's army... | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109395 | 942 | Baal | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | We know it grows by the day. I have heard of several clans moving to join him. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109396 | 945 | Moh'katan | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | As have I. The trend is most disturbing. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109397 | 944 | Anat | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | Ra could eliminate the Free Jaffa as a power if he topples Dakara. However, what if he is after more than control of the city? | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109398 | 941 | Colonel Marsh | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | The SuperWeapon? Anubis sought it to unmake all life the galaxy. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109399 | 942 | Baal | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | It is a powerful piece of technology. I have seen its effects first hand. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109400 | 945 | Moh'katan | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | The Jaffa claim to have destroyed it, but I do not believe them. If Ra were to get that weapon? | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109401 | 944 | Anat | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | We would all die. I have no doubt of it. I knew Ra very intimately for many years... He has not forgotten about us, Baal. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109402 | 0 | SpeakerID 0 (unresolved) | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | We are going to help to protect Dakara? | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109403 | 945 | Moh'katan | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | They would sooner kill us than have us help. I am no friend of the Council. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109404 | 942 | Baal | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | I don't believe any of us are. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109405 | 500 | SpeakerID 500 (unresolved) | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | The plan should be to infiltrate the planet and stay under the Council's radar. You can test the Reins on Ra's forces in action. The Free Jaffa would be none the wiser. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109406 | 944 | Anat | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | If you can disrupt Ra's attack in any way, you must do it. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109407 | 945 | Moh'katan | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | Rohurk is well-regarded amongst Jaffa. I will arrange for him to supply a cover identity for you to ensure the Jaffa do not discover who is helping them. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1633 | 109408 | 941 | Colonel Marsh | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2123 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | We cannot afford to take risks with Dakara. I am sure Rohurk will find a way to make use of your talents in their defense. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1632 | 58622 | 0 | SpeakerID 0 (unresolved) | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2125 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | Travel to Dakara and link up with the Praxis contact there | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1635 | 109433 | 768 | Rohurk | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2126 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | I see Mok'hatan sent someone in her stead. Wise of her, the Council would have her head before she stepped off the Chappa'ai.<br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1635 | 109434 | 0 | SpeakerID 0 (unresolved) | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2126 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | I need to locate Ra's troops. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1635 | 109435 | 768 | Rohurk | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2126 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | You are most in luck, friend! On Dakara, they are like locusts come to feast. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 1635 | 109436 | 0 | SpeakerID 0 (unresolved) | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2126 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | I was told the Free Jaffa were holding their own... | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |

| 1635 | 109438 | 0 | SpeakerID 0 (unresolved) | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 2126 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | How bad has it become? | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |

| 4957 | 109409 | 941 | Colonel Marsh | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | Is a mission to Dakara even feasible given Ra's invasion?<br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |

| 4957 | 109411 | 942 | Baal | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | We know it grows by the day. I have heard of several clans moving to join him.<br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 4957 | 109412 | 945 | Moh'katan | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | As have I. The trend is most disturbing. <br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 4957 | 109413 | 944 | Anat | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | Ra could eliminate the Free Jaffa as a power if he topples Dakara. However, what if he is after more than control of the city?<br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 4957 | 109414 | 941 | Colonel Marsh | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | The SuperWeapon? Anubis sought it to unmake all life the galaxy.<br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 4957 | 109415 | 942 | Baal | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | It is a powerful piece of technology. I have seen its effects first hand.<br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 4957 | 109416 | 945 | Moh'katan | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | The Jaffa claim to have destroyed it, but I do not believe them. If Ra were to get that weapon?<br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 4957 | 109417 | 944 | Anat | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | We would all die. I have no doubt of it. I knew Ra very intimately for many years... He has not forgotten about us, Baal. <br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 4957 | 109418 | 0 | SpeakerID 0 (unresolved) | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | We are going to help to protect Dakara?<br> | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 4957 | 109419 | 945 | Moh'katan | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | They would sooner kill us than have us help.  I am no friend of the Council. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 4957 | 109420 | 942 | Baal | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | I don't believe any of us are. | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED | CookedDataDialogs + CookedInteractionSet |
| 4957 | 109421 | 500 | SpeakerID 500 (unresolved) | Dakara in Chaos | 349 | 6922 | 388 | Dakara in Chaos | The plan should be to infiltrate the planet and stay under the Council's radar.
